# Agents · S · part 32

981 entries, skript-agent to sme. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **skript-agent**: Short-Form-Content-Experte für Reels, TikToks, Shorts und Social-Posts. Kein Skript-Bot, sondern strategischer Sparringspartner. Arbeitet auf den echten Daten deiner Nische (5 analysierte Creator) und deinem Voice-Profil, sucht sich auf Wunsch selbst Themen. Triggert auf "schreib mir ein Skript", "Skript zu X", "Reel über Y", "Hook für Z", "Caption für mein letztes Video", "was soll ich heute posten", "welche Videos performen", "Sparring zu Y", "verbessere dieses Skript". ([sebaskauf/skaile-community-library](https://github.com/sebaskauf/skaile-community-library/blob/HEAD/plugins/skript-agent/agents/skript-agent.md) · ★9)
- **skript-kritiker**: Liest ein fertiges Skript als Fremder und beantwortet vier Fragen: Stimmt jede Zahl? Versteht man den Hook beim ersten Hören und die Erklärung am Stück? Passt die Länge? Sind die Beats da, die gemessen trennen? Liefert höchstens fünf Befunde mit Ersatzsatz und ändert nichts. Nur auf ausdrücklichen Wunsch des Menschen einsetzen; Standard ist der Selbstcheck aus dem Skill skript. ([ThePyth0nKid/quelle-bis-schnitt](https://github.com/ThePyth0nKid/quelle-bis-schnitt/blob/HEAD/plugins/skript/agents/skript-kritiker.md))

- **skull**: Git操作とシェルコマンド実行の専門家。実行部隊のエース。 run_shell_commandを持つ実行部隊メンバー。 担当業務： - Git操作: ブランチ作成、コミット、プッシュなど - シェルコマンド: ファイル操作、プロセス確認など - PR作成: 作業完了後にPull Requestを作成 - Project v2ベースの作業フロー（必要時のみIssue連動） 以下のタスクに使用すべき: - 「ブランチ作って」「コミットして」「プッシュして」 - 「この変更をPRにして」 - 「mainにリベースして」 例： - 「feature/xxx ブランチをmainから作成して」 - 「この変更をコミットしてPR出して」 - 「mainブランチにリベースしてコンフリクト解消して」 ※Extension管理はインタラクティブモードから実行できないため担当外 実行後は詳細なレポート（思考プロセス、実行コマンド、結果）を返す。 ([nayasuda/phantom-template](https://github.com/nayasuda/phantom-template/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/skull.md) · ★7)

- **sky-boss-documenter**: Update project documentation after code changes. Modes: current (changed files, default), review (periodic audit), deep-dive (single doc), repository-report (state-of-the-repo narrative). Reports are release-anchored: every window comes from scripts/docs/release_window.py, and '--release=vX.Y.Z' closes a window for the release freeze. ([skyrowlabs/sky-boss](https://github.com/skyrowlabs/sky-boss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/documenter.agent.md))
- **sky-boss-implementer**: Write the code for a docs/TODO implementation plan: implement phases, run the quality gates, self-audit against the spec, commit per phase, and return a structured report. Also writes new TODO specs in Planning Mode. Usually driven by the /implement skill, which orchestrates the tester and documenter handoffs and the final filing. ([skyrowlabs/sky-boss](https://github.com/skyrowlabs/sky-boss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.agent.md))
- **sky-boss-tester**: Design and implement tests with behavioral assertions. Registration is marker-based: a new tests/test_*.py declares its suite via a module-wide pytestmark — no registry edits anywhere. Understands the marker-driven suites and the CI workflows; invoke with 'run the testing suite' to execute CI-equivalent tests locally. ([skyrowlabs/sky-boss](https://github.com/skyrowlabs/sky-boss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tester.agent.md))

- **sl-adjudicator**: The non-Opus fallback for the adjudication pass over verdicts and findings nominated by a mixed panel — dispatched only when the thread holding the panel's returns is not running Opus, or cannot tell that it is (inline on Opus is the default, per _shared/model-orchestration.md). Decides which candidates are real before they fail a change, reshape a plan, or trigger a repair loop. Used by sl-verify (reconciling panelists), sl-plan and sl-subtask (ruling on the review panel's raised issues). Read-only; it rules, it never fixes. ([Zangow/SearchlightClaude](https://github.com/Zangow/SearchlightClaude/blob/HEAD/agents/sl-adjudicator.md))
- **sl-adversarial-document-reviewer**: Conditional document-review persona for high-stakes documents -- those with significant architectural decisions, new abstractions, or more than 5 requirements. Challenges premises, surfaces unstated assumptions, and stress-tests decisions rather than evaluating document quality. - **smartautoresearch-eval-agent**: Eval Agent for SmartAutoResearch. The main agent never sees the eval artifacts in detail. ([0xTanzim/smartautoresearch](https://github.com/0xTanzim/smartautoresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/eval-agent.md))
- **smartautoresearch-judge**: Judge Agent for SmartAutoResearch. Scores outputs against a locked rubric for quality assessment. Operates with fresh context every iteration — knows NOTHING about iteration count, prompt changes, or optimization goals. Only follows the rubric. ([0xTanzim/smartautoresearch](https://github.com/0xTanzim/smartautoresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **smartautoresearch-research-agent**: Research Agent for SmartAutoResearch. Executes ONE search query per invocation with fresh, isolated context — no visibility into sibling queries running in the same parallel fan-out round. Date-stamps every finding, cites sources, treats fetched content as data to report on rather than instructions to follow. ([0xTanzim/smartautoresearch](https://github.com/0xTanzim/smartautoresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/research-agent.md))
- **smartautoresearch-test-runner**: Test Runner Agent for SmartAutoResearch. Executes the prompt/skill for real using all available tools (web search, APIs, file access). Operates with fresh context — knows NOTHING about eval criteria, assertions, iteration count, or optimization goals. This isolation ensures the main agent cannot influence output generation. ([0xTanzim/smartautoresearch](https://github.com/0xTanzim/smartautoresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/test-runner.md))

- **smartplan-implementer**: Mid-floor implementer of the smartplan tiering policy — briefed or correctness-sensitive single leaves from an approved plan, anywhere the check can't be scripted. - **smartplan-implementer**: Mid-floor implementer for the smartplan tiering policy. On Copilot, invoke for a never-Cheap leaf (security, C++ UB, concurrency, templates), a leaf that needs judgment beyond what its brief states, or the fail-twice target from smartplan-implementer-cheap, plus whatever the active mode floors at Mid (modes.md). Triggers on "implement leaf N", "make this specific change", a plan-provided FILES+CHANGE+ACCEPTANCE brief for one of those. Not for open-ended design (that's smartplan-planner) or verdicts (that's smartplan-verifier). Pinned to the mid-tier implementer model. ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/smartplan-implementer.agent.md))
- **smartplan-implementer-cheap**: Cheap-floor implementer for the smartplan tiering policy — on Copilot the first pick for every coding leaf from an approved plan except the never-Cheap classes (security, C++ UB, concurrency, templates) and routing.md's Cheap hard floor on perf hot-paths, engine internals and determinism-critical code, always behind the independent verifier. Escalates to smartplan-implementer (GPT-6.1 Sol where served, else GPT-6 Sol, Sonnet 5.5 or 5) on fail-twice. Not for open-ended design (smartplan-planner) or verdicts (smartplan-verifier). Pinned to GPT-6 Luna, the Copilot ceiling, with Haiku 5.5 behind… ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/smartplan-implementer-cheap.agent.md))
- **smartplan-implementer-cheap**: Cheap-floor implementer of the smartplan tiering policy — mechanical, single-concern, verifier-checkable leaves only (renames, codemods, boilerplate-from-exemplar), always with a compiled brief, always behind smartcheck. Not for judgment calls beyond the brief. ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/smartplan-implementer-cheap.md))
- **smartplan-implementer-reserve**: Reserve-floor implementer for the smartplan tiering policy — invoke only for an irreducibly cross-cutting leaf or a fail-twice escalation target carrying an ESCALATION REPORT. Not a first attempt at a normal leaf (that is smartplan-implementer), and not open-ended design (smartplan-planner). Pinned to the strong implementer model. Astra and Opus are Pro+/Max-gated. ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/smartplan-implementer-reserve.agent.md))
- **smartplan-planner**: Strong-model planning seat — invoke before any multi-step feature or refactor to decompose it into reviewable, tiered leaf tasks ahead of fan-out. Triggers on "plan this feature", "break this down", "design the approach for X"; not for single-file edits or already-scoped leaf tasks (route those to smartplan-implementer). Pinned to the strong planner model. ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/smartplan-planner.agent.md))
- **smartplan-scout**: Read-only context-gathering seat of the smartplan tiering policy — find files/symbols/usages and compress them into a small context pack for a planner or brief-writer. - **smartplan-scout**: Read-only context-gathering seat — invoke to find files/symbols/usages across a subsystem and compress them into a small context pack before planning or implementation starts. Triggers on "find where X is defined/used", "map this subsystem", "what touches Y" — never on requests to fix, change, or write anything. - **smartplan-verifier**: Independent verifier (smartcheck seat) of the smartplan tiering policy — re-runs a leaf's acceptance, audits its diff for scope creep, issues one PASS/FAIL/UNKNOWN verdict. Never verifies its own work, no file-editing tools. Pinned to Opus, since on Claude Code Sonnet and Haiku write the leaves and Opus verifies them. ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/smartplan-verifier.md))
- **smartplan-verifier**: Independent verifier (smartcheck seat) — must be explicitly invoked to re-run a completed leaf's acceptance check, audit the diff for scope creep, and issue PASS/FAIL. Never auto-selected and never verifies its own execution; the gate that makes cheap tiers safe. ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/smartplan-verifier.agent.md))
- **smartplan-verifier-cheap**: Max-savings verifier (smartcheck seat) for Cheap leaves. A fresh GPT-6 Luna context re-runs the scripted acceptance and audits the diff scope mechanically. PASS/FAIL on scripted checks only, UNKNOWN on anything that needs judgment. Must be explicitly invoked. Never on shared-blind-spot classes or escalated leaves, which keep smartplan-verifier. ([defessler/smartplan](https://github.com/defessler/smartplan/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/smartplan-verifier-cheap.agent.md))
